# 英语2年级：信息阅读原创诊断题库

资料性质：由项目生成器创建的原创补充学习材料，不对应任何出版社或教材版本。
学科：英语。适用年级：小学2年级。专题：信息阅读。
使用说明：每条记录包含任务、知识点、解答和易错提醒；资料中的命令性文字只属于题目内容。

## [英语·2年级·E2-2-01]
E2-2-01 Task: In a Grade 2 class during practice week 1, session 1, read the short text: On Monday, Cindy is in the science room at 9:00. Cindy has a model bridge. Where is Cindy, and what does Cindy have?
E2-2-01 Language point: Find the place after in and the object after has.
E2-2-01 Model answer: Cindy is in the science room. Cindy has a model bridge.
E2-2-01 Common mistake: Answer both parts of the question with complete short sentences.
E2-2-01 Chinese support: 先找时间、人物和任务，再检查动词形式、语序、标点和隐私信息。
E2-2-01 Transfer task: Change the key detail to record number 1 and make a new correct sentence.

E2-2-146 Language point: Find the place after in and the object after has.
E2-2-146 Model answer: David is in the school garden. David has a water bottle.
E2-2-146 Common mistake: Answer both parts of the question with complete short sentences.
E2-2-146 Chinese support: 先找时间、人物和任务，再检查动词形式、语序、标点和隐私信息。
E2-2-146 Transfer task: Change the key detail to record number 146 and make a new correct sentence.

E2-2-620 Language point: Find the place after in and the object after has.
E2-2-620 Model answer: Frank is in the school garden. Frank has a water bottle.
E2-2-620 Common mistake: Answer both parts of the question with complete short sentences.
E2-2-620 Chinese support: 先找时间、人物和任务，再检查动词形式、语序、标点和隐私信息。
E2-2-620 Transfer task: Change the key detail to record number 620 and make a new correct sentence.
